Calculating for x=3:
Offset = 0xA0000000 + 8*(3-1) = 0xA0000000 + 16 = 0xA0000010

The fact states that the "extmod" field of the "bcr3" register in the "fsmc" peripheral is "read-write". To verify this, I examined the provided search results, which contain detailed information about the "bcr3" register. 

In the extracted content, the "extmod" field (or "EXTMOD" bit) is discussed under the section describing the "FSMC_BCRx" register's bits. It states that "Bit 14 EXTMOD: Extended mode enable" can be set to 0 or 1, and it explains its functionality. The content states that "Address offset: 0xA000 0000 + 0x04 + 8 * (x – 1), x = 1..4)," which allows us to calculate the offset for register 2:

For x=2:
Address offset = 0xA000000 + 0x04 + 8 * (2 - 1)
= 0xA000000 + 0x04 + 8 * 1
= 0xA000000 + 0x04 + 0x8
= 0xA000004 + 0x8
= 0xA00000C

The datasheet explicitly states the reset value of the register as 0x0FFF FFFF, and the address offset is calculated as above. In the initial content, the `btr3` register is described with several bit fields, including `BUSTURN[3:0]` at bits 19-16. Calculating for "bwtr2": 
- The general formula is 0xA0000000 + 0x104 + 8 * (2 - 1) = 0xA0000000 + 0x104 + 8 = 0xA0000000 + 0x104 + 0x8 = 0xA0000000 + 0x10C.

Thus, the address offset for "bwtr2" is 0x10C, matching the given value to validate.

The fact to be validated is that the address offset of the register "bwtr4" in the "fsmc" peripheral is 0x11C.

From the search results, I see that the memory bank timing registers (FSMC_BTRx, FSMC_BWTRx) have their address offset calculated as:

> Address offset: 0xA000 0000 + 0x104 + 8 * (x – 1), x = 1...4

This formula indicates that these registers for each bank (1..4) are at specific addresses relative to a base address of 0xA0000000, with an offset addition of 0x104 plus 8 times the bank index minus one.

Since "bwtr4" pertains to the FSMC_BWTR4 register, corresponding to bank 4, I can compute its address offset as:

Base offset for bank 4: 0xA0000000 + 0x104 + 8*(4 - 1) = 0xA0000000 + 0x104 + 8*3

Calculating:

- 8*3 = 24 or 0x18

- So total offset from 0xA0000000: 0x104 + 0x18 = 0x11C

- Therefore, the address offset for "bwtr4" in bank 4 is 0xA0000000 + 0x11C

Given that, the relative offset from the peripheral base address is 0x11C.

The fact specifies an address offset of 0x11C, matching the computed value from the datasheet.

Considering that the initial address offset calculation uses the same bank number (4) and the same offset calculation, I find consistent information validating the specified offset.

Loosening the constraints, the 0x11C value corresponds directly to the offset for "bwtr4" in the "fsmc" peripheral's memory map.

Therefore, I am confident that the address offset for "bwtr4" in "fsmc" is 0x11C.

I am 100% certain with this reasoning.
